### `exp008_armC_unified_mem_freeze_incident` — recovered ()
_2026-06-08T16:23:12.700434Z_

- **Expected:** safe live run; instead a near-machine-hang -- arm C abandoned on this box
- **Observed:** INCIDENT: arm C (util 0.46, ~48GiB weights) launch on the GB10 (121GiB UNIFIED mem) thrashed the system -- SSH + UI tracking went down. Root cause: unified memory feeds GPU AND OS; loading a 2nd ~48GiB model alongside the 48GiB production gemma starved the OS. Kernel OOM-killed qat-eval-scratch -> box recovered (no reboot, 24d uptime). Damage: NONE critical -- production gemma :8000 healthy, GPU clean (only gemma 49962MiB), 67GiB free. nara-sandbox container shows Up(unhealthy). qwen still stopped (needs docker start vllm-qwen). LESSON: arm C (unquantized 48GiB) is too tight to run safely alongside production gemma on unified memory; my util 0.46 guidance was too aggressive. STOP retrying arm C -> use arm B (15GiB gguf) or shelve the live run.

_Narrative (nara, apparatus_event):_
- **Intent:** safe live run; instead a near-machine-hang -- arm C abandoned on this box
- **Did:** INCIDENT: arm C (util 0.46, ~48GiB weights) launch on the GB10 (121GiB UNIFIED mem) thrashed the system -- SSH + UI tracking went down. Root cause: unified memory feeds GPU AND OS; loading a 2nd ~48GiB model alongside the 48GiB production g…
- **Observed:** status=recovered day= duration_ms=None fallback=None
- **Would do differently:** n/a — projected, not authored

### Apparatus — D-039 — Gemma 4 QAT evaluated vs the NVFP4 pin (DRAFT, pending exp008 live run)

**Status.** DRAFT — disposition PENDING the exp008 live run. Outcome: **[pending exp008 RESULTS.md]**.

**Trigger.** Gemma 4 QAT (quantization-aware training) is a quant variant of the *exact* model the orchestrator already runs (Gemma 4 26B-A4B).
